{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01T2L1xHqzYty4zxLz7Cub4X","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Parallelized bootstrap probe with joblib.\\\\n\\\\nRuns n_seeds bootstrap iterations across 4 cross-lingual directions,\\\\nparallelized over seeds. Computes per-layer balanced_accuracy stats\\\\nand paired asymmetry CI (zh_en - en_zh).\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport time\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom joblib import Parallel, delayed\\\\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\\\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import balanced_accuracy_score\\\\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_activations(path):\\\\n    data = torch.load(path, map_location=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\", weights_only=True)\\\\n    n_train = data[\\\\\\\"split_sizes\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"train\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    acts = data[\\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    if acts.dtype == torch.bfloat16:\\\\n        acts = acts.float()\\\\n    acts_np = acts.numpy()\\\\n    labels_np = data[\\\\\\\"labels\\\\\\\"].numpy()\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"train_X\\\\\\\": acts_np[:n_train],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"test_X\\\\\\\": acts_np[n_train:],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"train_y\\\\\\\": labels_np[:n_train],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"test_y\\\\\\\": labels_np[n_train:],\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef single_bootstrap(seed, train_X_all, train_y, test_X_all, test_y, n_layers):\\\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\\\n    n = len(train_y)\\\\n    idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\\\\n    y_boot = train_y[idx]\\\\n\\\\n    layer_accs = np.empty(n_layers)\\\\n    for layer in range(n_layers):\\\\n        X_tr = train_X_all[:, layer, :]\\\\n        X_tr_boot = X_tr[idx]\\\\n        X_te = test_X_all[:, layer, :]\\\\n\\\\n        scaler = StandardScaler()\\\\n        X_tr_s = scaler.fit_transform(X_tr_boot)\\\\n        X_te_s = scaler.transform(X_te)\\\\n\\\\n        clf = LogisticRegression(\\\\n            class_weight=\\\\\\\"balanced\\\\\\\", max_iter=1000, solver=\\\\\\\"lbfgs\\\\\\\",\\\\n            random_state=seed,\\\\n        )\\\\n        clf.fit(X_tr_s, y_boot)\\\\n        pred = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01N7WrNEpahNJ9x9DDc3EnGE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016xRyVRcdzrLJrouLeqKFZz","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-14391","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nimport json, numpy as np, torch\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.metrics import balanced_accuracy_score\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n\nfor model_name, path in [('llama31', '/root/clkc_mech_interp/data/llama31/en_activations.pt'),\n                          ('gemma2_v2', '/root/clkc_mech_interp/data/gemma2_v2/en_activations.pt')]:\n    data = torch.load(path, map_location='cpu', weights_only=True)\n    n_train = data['split_sizes']['train']\n    acts = data['activations']\n    if acts.dtype == torch.bfloat16:\n        acts = acts.float()\n    acts_np = acts.numpy()\n    labels_np = data['labels'].numpy()\n    train_X, test_X = acts_np[:n_train], acts_np[n_train:]\n    train_y, test_y = labels_np[:n_train], labels_np[n_train:]\n    n_layers = train_X.shape[1]\n    results = []\n    for layer in range(n_layers):\n        scaler = StandardScaler()\n        X_tr = scaler.fit_transform(train_X[:, layer, :])\n        X_te = scaler.transform(test_X[:, layer, :])\n        clf = LogisticRegression(class_weight='balanced', max_iter=1000, solver='lbfgs', random_state=42)\n        clf.fit(X_tr, train_y)\n        pred = clf.predict(X_te)\n        ba = balanced_accuracy_score(test_y, pred)\n        results.append({'layer': layer, 'balanced_accuracy': round(ba, 4)})\n    out = {'model': model_name, 'n_layers': n_layers, 'per_layer': results}\n    with open(f'/root/clkc_mech_interp/data/{model_name}/probe_per_layer.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(out, f, indent=2)\n    print(f'{model_name}: {n_layers} layers, peak={max(r[\\\"balanced_accuracy\\\"] for r in results)} at L{max(results, key=lambda r: r[\\\"balanced_accuracy\\\"])[\\\"layer\\\"]}')\nprint('Done')\n\"\n","account":"root","warn":true},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":591,"cache_read_input_tokens":73939,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":591},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f6cc1f73-3fd1-4f57-a23a-f37d262fecff","uuid":"19805fec-1387-459a-80cb-a35f6d0dc69d"}

Let me summarize:\n\n**Llama 3.1-8B (32 layers)**:\n- Per-layer probe: peak at L15 (0.9223), plateau ~L9-L16\n- Steering p-values at alpha=+10:\n  - L12: 0.352 (NS)\n  - L13: 0.000337 *** (significant!)\n  - L14: 0.00224 ** (significant!)\n  - L16: 0.005085 ** (marginally significant after Bonferroni)\n  - L18: 0.098 (NS)\n  - L28: 0.284 (NS)\n- After applying Bonferroni correction with 6 tests (threshold = 0.00833), only L13, L14, and L16 survive as significant\n\n**Qwen3-8B (36 layers)**:\n- Per-layer probe peaks at L22 (0.7481)\n- Steering at alpha=+10 shows no significant effects across L13-L15\n\n**Gemma2-9B (42 layers)**:\n- Per-layer probe shows strongest signal at L7 (0.8977) with secondary peaks at L18 and L26\n- Steering results at alpha=+10 remain non-significant across L16-L18\n\nNow I'm planning the visualization for Figures 5 and 6, starting with a three-panel dot plot showing -log10(p-values) per layer with the Bonferroni threshold marked. For Llama's L13, I need to determine the optimal alpha value—alpha=+10 gives p=0.000337, so I'm checking what other alpha values yield.\n\nLooking across all alpha values for each layer to find the minimum p-value, I see Llama L12 ranges from 0.085 to 0.862 depending on alpha, while L13 shows much stronger effects with the minimum p-value approaching zero at alpha=+10. Now I'm applying Bonferroni correction with alpha=+10 as the primary hypothesis, testing across 6 layers with a corrected threshold of 0.00833. Looking at the results, layers 13, 14, and 16 show significant p-values below this threshold, while layers 12, 18, and 28 don't reach significance.\n\nChecking the same analysis for Qwen and Gemma models—none of their tested layers show significance at this threshold. matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\nfrom matplotlib.lines import Line2D\n\nNAVY = '#1B2838'\nSLATE = '#3D5A80'\nTEAL = '#5BA4B5'\nCORAL = '#E07A5F'\nEN_BLUE = '#4A90D9'\nZH_RED = '#E07A5F'\nBG = '#FFFFFF'\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'DejaVu Serif'],\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'axes.labelsize': 10,\n    'axes.titlesize': 11,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 8.5,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 8.5,\n    'figure.facecolor': BG,\n    'axes.facecolor': BG,\n    'axes.edgecolor': NAVY,\n    'xtick.color': NAVY,\n    'ytick.color': NAVY,\n    'axes.labelcolor': NAVY,\n    'text.color': NAVY,\n})\n\n# ─── DATA ───\n\n# Steering p-values at alpha=+10 (Fisher exact test)\nllama_steering = {\n    12: 0.351771,\n    13: 0.000337,\n    14: 0.00224,\n    16: 0.005085,\n    18: 0.098394,\n    28: 0.284122,\n}\n\nqwen_steering = {\n    13: 0.247126,\n    14: 1.0,\n    15: 1.0,\n}\n\ngemma_steering = {\n    16: 0.067119,\n    17: 1.0,\n    18: 0.723312,\n}\n\n# Effect sizes (steering_flip_rate - control_flip_rate) at alpha=+10\nllama_effect = {\n    12: 0.1891 - 0.1493,\n    13: 0.2836 - 0.1343,\n    14: 0.2786 - 0.1493,\n    16: 0.1841 - 0.0846,\n    18: 0.1294 - 0.0746,\n    28: 0.0299 - 0.0100,\n}\n\nqwen_effect = {\n    13: 0.0229 - 0.0,\n    14: 0.0229 - 0.0153,\n    15: 0.0229 - 0.0229,\n}\n\ngemma_effect = {\n    16: 0.0058 - 0.0409,\n    17: 0.0234 - 0.0175,\n    18: 0.0292 - 0.0175,\n}\n\n# Per-layer probe balanced accuracy\nllama_probe = [0.7799, 0.8198, 0.8599, 0.8699, 0.8549, 0.8473, 0.8474, 0.8549, 0.8449,\n               0.8999, 0.8949, 0.9048, 0.9174, 0.8998, 0.9173, 0.9223, 0.9149, 0.8923,\n               0.9073, 0.8898, 0.8898, 0.8847, 0.8973, 0.8874, 0.8648, 0.8423, 0.8498,\n               0.8498, 0.8523, 0.8624, 0.8549, 0.8624]\n\nqwen_probe = [0.5725, 0.645, 0.6336, 0.6298, 0.7061, 0.7023, 0.6603, 0.7061, 0.6908,\n              0.6718, 0.6641, 0.7023, 0.687, 0.7176, 0.7099, 0.729, 0.7099, 0.7328,\n              0.7137, 0.6985, 0.729, 0.729, 0.7481, 0.7366, 0.7176, 0.7061, 0.729,\n              0.7061, 0.7214, 0.6908, 0.6908, 0.7214, 0.6718, 0.6947, 0.6832, 0.7023]\n\ngemma_probe = [0.7836, 0.848, 0.8333, 0.8392, 0.8333, 0.848, 0.8684, 0.8977, 0.8713,\n               0.8743, 0.8421, 0.8363, 0.8538, 0.883, 0.8626, 0.8655, 0.8801, 0.8889,\n               0.8947, 0.886, 0.883, 0.8801, 0.883, 0.8772, 0.8655, 0.8772, 0.8918,\n               0.883, 0.8655, 0.8567, 0.8538, 0.8538, 0.8304, 0.8216, 0.8246, 0.8099,\n               0.7924, 0.7953, 0.7953, 0.7953, 0.8012, 0.7924]\n\n# Bonferroni threshold: 6 tests for Llama, 3 for Qwen/Gemma\nbonferroni_llama = 0.05 / 6  # 0.00833\nbonferroni_qwen = 0.05 / 3   # 0.01667\nbonferroni_gemma = 0.05 / 3   # 0.01667\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# FIGURE 5: Steering Results — Dot plot with significance\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\nfig5, axes5 = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14, 4), dpi=300)\nfig5.subplots_adjust(wspace=0.28, left=0.06, right=0.97, top=0.88, bottom=0.15)\n\nmodels_steer = [\n    ('Llama-3.1-8B', llama_steering, llama_effect, bonferroni_llama, 6),\n    ('Qwen3-8B', qwen_steering, qwen_effect, bonferroni_qwen, 3),\n    ('Gemma-2-9B', gemma_steering, gemma_effect, bonferroni_gemma, 3),\n]\n\nfor idx, (name, steer_p, effect, bonf, n_tests) in enumerate(models_steer):\n    ax = axes5[idx]\n    layers = sorted(steer_p.keys())\n    pvals = [steer_p[l] for l in layers]\n    effects = [effect[l] for l in layers]\n    neg_log_p = [-np.log10(max(p, 1e-10)) for p in pvals]\n    threshold = -np.log10(bonf)\n\n    ax.axhline(y=threshold, color=SLATE, linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.6, zorder=1)\n    ax.text(layers[-1] + 0.3, threshold + 0.08, f'$\\\\alpha$=0.05/{n_tests}',\n            fontsize=7, color=SLATE, va='bottom', ha='right')\n\n    for i, (l, nlp, ef) in enumerate(zip(layers, neg_log_p, effects)):\n        sig = nlp > threshold\n        size = max(40, min(200, abs(ef) * 1200))\n        if sig:\n            ax.scatter(l, nlp, s=size, color=CORAL, edgecolors=NAVY,\n                       linewidth=0.6, zorder=5, marker='o')\n        else:\n            ax.scatter(l, nlp, s=size, color='white', edgecolors=SLATE,\n                       linewidth=1.2, zorder=5, marker='o')\n\n    ax.set_xlabel('Layer', fontsize=10)\n    ax.set_xticks(layers)\n    ax.set_xticklabels([f'L{l}' for l in layers])\n\n    if idx == 0:\n        ax.set_ylabel('$-\\\\log_{10}(p)$', fontsize=10)\n\n    ax.set_title(name, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold', pad=8)\n    ax.set_ylim(-0.15, max(max(neg_log_p) + 0.5, threshold + 0.8))\n    ax.tick_params(axis='both', which='both', length=3)\n\nlegend_elements = [\n    Line2D([0], [0], marker='o', color='none', markerfacecolor=CORAL,\n           markeredgecolor=NAVY, markersize=8, label='Significant'),\n    Line2D([0], [0], marker='o', color='none', markerfacecolor='white',\n           markeredgecolor=SLATE, markersize=8, label='Not significant'),\n    Line2D([0], [0], color=SLATE, linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, label='Bonferroni threshold'),\n]\nfig5.legend(handles=legend_elements, loc='upper center', ncol=3,\n            fontsize=8.5, frameon=False, bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.99))\n\nfor fmt in ['png', 'pdf']:\n    fig5.savefig(f'/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod42/projects/clkc_mech_interp/figures/paper/fig5_steering.{fmt}',\n                 dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight', facecolor=BG)\nplt.close(fig5)\nprint(\"Figure 5 saved.\")\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# FIGURE 6: Detection-Decision Dissociation\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\nfig6, axes6 = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14, 4.2), dpi=300)\nfig6.subplots_adjust(wspace=0.38, left=0.06, right=0.95, top=0.86, bottom=0.15)\n\nmodels_dissoc = [\n    ('Llama-3.1-8B', llama_probe, llama_steering, bonferroni_llama, 32),\n    ('Qwen3-8B', qwen_probe, qwen_steering, bonferroni_qwen, 36),\n    ('Gemma-2-9B', gemma_probe, gemma_steering, bonferroni_gemma, 42),\n]\n\nfor idx, (name, probe, steer_p, bonf, n_layers) in enumerate(models_dissoc):\n    ax = axes6[idx]\n    layers_all = np.arange(n_layers)\n\n    # Probe accuracy line\n    ax.plot(layers_all, probe, color=TEAL, linewidth=1.8, zorder=3, label='Probe accuracy')\n    ax.fill_between(layers_all, 0.5, probe, alpha=0.06, color=TEAL, zorder=1)\n\n    peak_layer = int(np.argmax(probe))\n    peak_val = max(probe)\n    ax.scatter(peak_layer, peak_val, s=50, color=TEAL, edgecolors=NAVY,\n               linewidth=0.6, zorder=6, marker='D')\n\n    # Causal significance markers on probe curve\n    steer_layers = sorted(steer_p.keys())\n    threshold = -np.log10(bonf)\n\n    has_sig = False\n    for sl in steer_layers:\n        nlp = -np.log10(max(steer_p[sl], 1e-10))\n        sig = nlp > threshold\n        probe_val = probe[sl]\n        if sig:\n            ax.scatter(sl, probe_val, s=120, color=CORAL, edgecolors=NAVY,\n                       linewidth=0.8, zorder=7, marker='*')\n            has_sig = True\n        else:\n            ax.scatter(sl, probe_val, s=30, color=SLATE, edgecolors=SLATE,\n                       linewidth=0.6, zorder=5, marker='x', alpha=0.5)\n\n    # Annotations for Llama panel\n    if idx == 0:\n        # Causal window highlight\n        ax.axvspan(12.5, 16.5, alpha=0.06, color=CORAL, zorder=0)\n        # Detection peak annotation\n        ax.annotate('Detection\\npeak',\n                    xy=(peak_layer, peak_val), xytext=(peak_layer + 5, peak_val + 0.015),\n                    fontsize=7.5, color=TEAL, fontweight='bold',\n                    arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color=TEAL, lw=0.8),\n                    ha='center', va='bottom')\n        # Causal window
